This worklist renders the frozen source-callers.json inventory. It is an iteration plan, not an assertion that every source or execution path has been semantically audited. Caller sites are lexical candidates; inactive generate branches, duplicate mirror definitions, unflisted tests, and macro-dependent interfaces require elaboration before a reachability claim.

For each file, trace reset and disabled configuration, accepted transfer, normal completion, backpressure, recovery, simultaneous events, and identity reuse. Follow each caller through the next stored owner or architectural effect. Record contract, configuration, cycle distance, retained payload, pending credit, cancellation rule, source identity, and the earliest faithful discriminator. Apply the aggregate batch plan in retirement-plan.md; leave the item open if any caller or branch remains unreviewed.

The historical origin decision is retained as provenance only. COPY does not prove current byte identity or semantic equivalence, and REDESIGN does not prove completion. Files absent from the three recorded manifests may be tests, alternatives, or stale material; absence alone is not a deletion instruction.

The current inventory is schema hyp8-source-caller-inventory.v2. It supersedes the preserved pre-boundary-fix inventory, whose search could mistake a longer identifier beginning with a module name for a caller. Source hashes and review states are unchanged; only the lexical caller graph was corrected.
